## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- pipelines-components is a Kubernetes component.
- Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 8080.
- connecting to external services\: gcs, milvus, mlflow, openai, requests, s3, sqlite, urllib.
- runtime dependencies\: MLflow, S3, kubernetes, milvus-milvus, minio-service, registry-service.
- 47 Python package dependencies (47 required), categories\: 1 k8s-client, 1 observability, 2 serialization.
- Python k8s client with 29 API call(s) and 2 CRD kind reference(s).
- references components\: eval-hub (import, provider), kserve (provider).
